Detailed template:

```
asm volatile(
    /* Load constants and base pointers */
    "movl %[m], %%edi\n\t"
    "movl %[n], %%esi\n\t"
    "shlq $3, %%rsi\n\t"          // rsi = n*8
    "movq %[A], %%r10\n\t"        // r10 = A row pointer
    "movq %[x], %%rcx\n\t"
    "leaq (%%rcx,%%rsi), %%rdx\n\t" // rdx = xend
    "movq %[y], %%r8\n\t"
    "movq %[tmp], %%r9\n\t"

    /* y[0..n-1] = 0 */
    "movq %%r8, %%r11\n\t"
    "leaq (%%r8,%%rsi), %%rax\n\t"
    ".Lzero%=:\n\t"
    "movq $0, (%%r11)\n\t"
    "addq $8, %%r11\n\t"
    "cmpq %%rax, %%r11\n\t"
    "jne .Lzero%=\n\t"

    /* outer i = 0 */
    "xorl %%eax, %%eax\n\t"
    "cmpq %%rdi, %%rax\n\t"
    "jge .Lend%=\n\t"

    ".Louter%=:\n\t"
    /* tmp[i] = 0 */
    "movq $0, (%%r9,%%rax,8)\n\t"

    /* tmp[i] += A[i][j] * x[j] */
    "pxor %%xmm0, %%xmm0\n\t"
    "movq %%rcx, %%r11\n\t"
    ".Ldot%=:\n\t"
    "movsd (%%r10), %%xmm1\n\t"
    "mulsd (%%r11), %%xmm1\n\t"
    "addsd %%xmm1, %%xmm0\n\t"
    "addq $8, %%r10\n\t"
    "addq $8, %%r11\n\t"
    "cmpq %%rdx, %%r11\n\t"
    "jne .Ldot%=\n\t"

    "movsd %%xmm0, (%%r9,%%rax,8)\n\t"

    /* y[j] += A[i][j] * tmp[i] */
    "subq %%rsi, %%r10\n\t"
    "movq %%r8, %%r11\n\t"
    "leaq (%%r8,%%rsi), %%rdx\n\t"
    ".Ly%=:\n\t"
    "movsd (%%r11), %%xmm2\n\t"
    "movsd (%%r10), %%xmm1\n\t"
    "mulsd %%xmm0, %%xmm1\n\t"
    "addsd %%xmm1, %%xmm2\n\t"
    "movsd %%xmm2, (%%r11)\n\t"
    "addq $8, %%r10\n\t"
    "addq $8, %%r11\n\t"
    "cmpq %%rdx, %%r11\n\t"
    "jne .Ly%=\n\t"

    /* next i */
    "leaq (%%rcx,%%rsi), %%rdx\n\t"
    "incq %%rax\n\t"
    "cmpq %%rdi, %%rax\n\t"
    "jl .Louter%=\n\t"

    ".Lend%=:\n\t"
    :
    : [A] "m" (A), [x] "m" (x), [y] "m" (y), [tmp] "m" (tmp),
      [m] "i" (_PB_M), [n] "i" (_PB_N)
    : "memory", "cc", "rax", "rcx", "rdx", "rdi", "rsi", "r8", "r9", "r10", "r11",

);
```

Let's maybe produce final code:

```
asm volatile(
    "movq %[A], %%r10\n\t"
    "movq %[x], %%rcx\n\t"
    "movq %[y], %%r8\n\t"
    "movq %[tmp], %%r9\n\t"
    "movl %[m], %%edi\n\t"
    "movl %[n], %%esi\n\t"
    "shlq $3, %%rsi\n\t"
    "leaq (%%rcx,%%rsi), %%rdx\n\t"
    "movq %%r8, %%r11\n\t"
    "leaq (%%r8,%%rsi), %%rax\n\t"
    ".Lzero%=:\n\t"
    "movq $0, (%%r11)\n\t"
    "addq $8, %%r11\n\t"
    "cmpq %%rax, %%r11\n\t"
    "jne .Lzero%=\n\t"
    "xorl %%eax, %%eax\n\t"
    "cmpq %%rdi, %%rax\n\t"
    "jge .Lend%=\n\t"
    ".Louter%=:\n\t"
    "movq $0, (%%r9,%%rax,8)\n\t"
    "pxor %%xmm0, %%xmm0\n\t"
    "movq %%rcx, %%r11\n\t"
    ".Ldot%=:\n\t"
    "movsd (%%r10), %%xmm1\n\t"
    "mulsd (%%r11), %%xmm1\n\t"
    "addsd %%xmm1, %%xmm0\n\t"
    "addq $8, %%r10\n\t"
    "addq $8, %%r11\n\t"
    "cmpq %%rdx, %%r11\n\t"
    "jne .Ldot%=\n\t"
    "movsd %%xmm0, (%%r9,%%rax,8)\n\t"
    "subq %%rsi, %%r10\n\t"
    "movq %%r8, %%r11\n\t"
    "leaq (%%r8,%%rsi), %%rdx\n\t"
    ".Ly%=:\n\t"
    "movsd (%%r11), %%xmm2\n\t"
    "movsd (%%r10), %%xmm1\n\t"
    "mulsd %%xmm0, %%xmm1\n\t"
    "addsd %%xmm1, %%xmm2\n\t"
    "movsd %%xmm2, (%%r11)\n\t"
    "addq $8, %%r10\n\t"
    "addq $8, %%r11\n\t"
    "cmpq %%rdx, %%r11\n\t"
    "jne .Ly%=\n\t"
    "leaq (%%rcx,%%rsi), %%rdx\n\t"
    "incq %%rax\n\t"
    "cmpq %%rdi, %%rax\n\t"
    "jl .Louter%=\n\t"
    ".Lend%=:\n"
    :
    : [A] "m" (A),
      [x] "m" (x),
      [y] "m" (y),
      [tmp] "m" (tmp),
      [m] "i" (_PB_M),
      [n] "i" (_PB_N)
    : "memory", "cc", "rax", "rcx", "rdx", "rdi", "rsi",
      "r8", "r9", "r10", "r11", "xmm0", "xmm1", "xmm2"
);
```
